We comprehensively demonstrate ALLUDE's evaluation breadth through a two-pronged strategy: (1) using Latin Hypercube Sampling, we draw a representative subset from 5,400 configurations spanning 10 scene-object pairs, 9 weather conditions, 4 optimizers, 5 camera trajectories, and 3 detection models; (2) we stress-test existing attacks (CAMOU, RAUCA, FCA) under diverse weather conditions and continuous camera trajectories, revealing degradation of attack success across every attack, exposing evaluation gaps in prior work. Through ALLUDE's end-to-end differentiable rendering, adversarial attacks can be optimized against shifting real-world deployment conditions. Our cross-platform code is open source.

</details>


### [100] [Autoregressive B-Rep Shape Generation with Parametric Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17093)
*Dafei Qin,Rui Xu,Zeyu Shen,Kaichun Qiao,Hongyang Lin,Qixuan Zhang,Huaijin Pi,Lan Xu,Jingyi Yu,Wenping Wang,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ParaCAD是一种自回归框架，直接在原生参数化曲面上进行点云条件下B-Rep生成，通过表面中心化分词、UV域约束和全局曲面求交，实现了高几何精度和下游可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有B-Rep生成方法依赖均匀采样的点或网格表示，丢失了原生曲面类型和参数，限制了几何保真度和下游可用性。

Method: 提出表面中心化分词，显式编码每个面的曲面类型和连续参数；先生成带UV域约束的参数化曲面，再通过全局求交构建有效B-Rep。

Result: 在几何精度、鲁棒性、水密性和下游可用性方面均优于基于点的方法，生成的点云对齐高保真B-Rep。

Conclusion: ParaCAD实现了用户引导的重建，可无缝集成到现有3D生成流程中。

Abstract: Generative CAD modeling has broad design and application potential. Despite significant advances in Boundary Representation (B-Rep) generation, the dominant representation in CAD, existing methods largely depend on uniformly sampled point- or grid-based geometry representations, sacrificing native surface types and parameters and thereby limiting geometric fidelity and downstream usability. We present ParaCAD, an autoregressive framework for point-cloud-conditioned B-Rep generation that directly operates on native parametric surfaces. ParaCAD introduces a surface-centric tokenization that explicitly encodes each face by its exact surface type and continuous parameters, preserving the intrinsic semantics of CAD geometry. Our model first generates parametric surfaces with constrained UV domains, and then constructs a valid B-Rep by globally intersecting these surfaces to recover edges and vertices. ParaCAD places point-cloud-conditioned generation at the core of B-Rep synthesis, making it practical for user-guided reconstruction and seamless integration into existing 3D generation pipelines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ParaCAD produces accurate B-Reps with faithful point-cloud alignment, outperforming point-based baselines in geometric precision, robustness, watertightness and downstream usability.

</details>


### [101] [HarmoHOI: Harmonizing Appearance and 3D Motion for Multi-view Hand-Object Interaction Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17097)
*Lingwei Dang,Juntong Li,Zonghan Li,Hongwen Zhang,Liang An,Wei Min,Yebin Liu,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HarmoHOI是一个统一的扩散框架，通过联合建模多视角RGB视频和3D点轨迹，实现高质量的双手-物体交互合成，在视觉质量、运动合理性和多视角几何一致性上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型在处理手-物交互合成时，由于复杂的手部运动和遮挡问题，难以保证多视角一致性。核心挑战在于缺乏全局对齐的3D几何与运动信息。

Method: 提出混合多视角扩散Transformer（共建模RGB视频与3D点轨迹）、将点轨迹表示为伪视频以对齐2D隐空间、全局运动对齐扩散（优化粗糙点轨迹为度量尺度全局对齐的3D轨迹）、混合数据课程学习策略（从单视角数据迁移先验到多视角生成）。

Result: 在视觉质量、运动合理性和多视角几何一致性上均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 同时建模2D外观和3D运动并在去噪过程中协同演化，是解决多视角手-物交互合成一致性的有效范式。

Abstract: Hand-Object Interaction (HOI) synthesis is a cornerstone for animation production and embodied AI. Despite the strong priors of video foundation models, multi-view consistent HOI synthesis remains challenging due to complex hand motions and occlusions. We present HarmoHOI, a unified diffusion framework that jointly and harmoniously generates synchronized multi-view HOI videos and globally aligned 3D point tracks. Our core insight is that robust multi-view consistency fundamentally requires globally aligned 3D geometry and motion. To this end, we propose a Mixture of Multi-view Diffusion Transformer that co-models RGB videos and 3D point tracks. By representing point tracks as pseudo-videos, we align 3D geometric signals with the 2D latent space of foundation models, thereby minimizing the domain gap and easing adaptation of priors. To further ensure geometry consistency, we introduce Global Motion Aligning Diffusion, which refines coarse point tracks into metric-scale, globally aligned 3D trajectories. HarmoHOI enables on-the-fly co-evolution of 2D appearance and 3D motion during denoising. To overcome the scarcity of multi-view HOI data, we employ a hybrid data curriculum learning strategy that successfully transfers generic priors from single-view data to synchronized multi-view generation. Experimental results show that HarmoHOI achieves state-of-the-art performance in visual quality, motion plausibility, and multi-view geometric consistency. Project page available at https://droliven.github.io/HarmoHOI_project.

</details>


### [102] [DepthART: Scaling Foundation Monocular Depth to Tiny Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17099)
*Feng Xue,Wu Chen,Mingshuai Zhao,Guofeng Zhong,Anlong Ming,Haozhe Wang,Dianqiao Lei,Zhaowen Lin,Haiyang Zhang,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DepthART，一种面向边缘设备部署的紧凑型单目深度估计模型，通过抗偏置数据采样和相机条件微调策略，在极小模型上实现了与大型模型接近的零样本泛化性能和度量精度，同时支持在Jetson Nano等低算力设备上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型（如Metric3D、Depth Anything、UniDepth）在单目深度估计的跨场景泛化和度量尺度预测上取得了显著进步，但这些成果未能迁移到小模型上。小模型存在两个容量驱动的瓶颈：(i) 对数据集特定分布偏置的过拟合；(ii) 相机参数变化下度量适应的不稳定性。

Method: DepthART结合两种简单有效的策略：(1) 抗偏置数据采样方案，在相同训练预算下减少分布偏置；(2) 相机条件微调协议，冻结蒸馏后的编码器，根据相机内参条件调整度量尺度，同时更好地保留跨数据集泛化能力。

Result: 在各数据集上，DepthART在零样本泛化和度量精度上持续超越先前的小模型基线（如DepthART-S在NYUD v2上零样本δ₁=0.964），某些情况下接近大模型性能。DepthART-S在RTX A6000上达347/245 FPS（严格FP32，224²/448²），在Orin NX 8GB上达102 FPS（TF32），在Jetson Nano 4GB上超15 FPS（FP32）。

Conclusion: DepthART成功弥合了大型基础模型与小型部署模型之间的性能差距，为边缘设备上的高质量单目深度估计提供了可扩展的模型家族方案。

Abstract: Recent geometric foundation models (e.g., Metric3D, Depth Anything and UniDepth) have substantially improved monocular depth estimation (MDE) in both cross-scene generalization and metric-scale prediction, yet these gains have not translated to tiny models. We bridge this gap with DepthART (Depth Anything Rethought for Tiny Models), which is a compact MDE model for on-device deployment across diverse scenes. We first identify two capacity-driven bottlenecks in tiny models: (i) overfitting to dataset-specific distribution bias and (ii) unstable metric adaptation under camera shift, where full fine-tuning easily damages transferable geometry. Accordingly, DepthART combines two simple but effective strategies: a bias-resistant data sampling scheme to reduce distribution bias under the same training budget, and a camera-conditioned fine-tuning protocol that freezes the distilled encoder and adjusts metric scale conditioned on intrinsics while better preserving cross-dataset generalization. Across datasets, DepthART consistently surpasses previous tiny baselines in both zero-shot generalization and metric accuracy (e.g., zero-shot $δ_1$=0.964 for DepthART-S on NYUD v2), and in some cases approaches heavy models. We further provide a scalable model family, with DepthART-S reaching 347/245 FPS (strict FP32) on an RTX A6000 at $224^2/448^2$, 102 FPS (TF32) on a Orin NX 8GB, and over 15 FPS (FP32) on a Jetson Nano 4GB.

</details>


### [103] [The generator is the tracker: Multi-object tracking by painting persistent identity colours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17120)
*Haiyu Yang,Miel Hostens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索是否可以用视频生成器（22B参数文本-视频扩散模型）代替传统多目标跟踪中的检测-关联+外部状态框架，通过模型直接为每个目标生成持久且区分的颜色ID图，在无检测器、无跟踪器、无重识别模块的情况下达到40.3 HOTA，其中关联分数（44.1 AssA）超过原始基准所有跟踪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统MOT的固定范式（检测+关联+身份状态维护），探索是否可以将目标身份维护在像素层面，由视频生成器直接完成。

Method: 使用22B文本-视频扩散模型LTX-2.3，通过轻量级in-context LoRA微调，将RGB视频转化为每个行人具有持久统一颜色的ID图；长视频通过分窗链式生成，前一窗口尾部清洁后作为下一窗口条件；额外微调让模型学会延续已有颜色分配。

Result: DanceTrack测试集HOTA 40.3，AssA 44.1超过原始基准所有跟踪器；控制的消融表明生成机制本身关键（后处理关联HOTA仅18.2）；383个遮挡事件中重获取率42%，而外观基线为0%，包括超出时间上下文的间隙。

Conclusion: 首次证明生成式跟踪器可行，具有独特的反向错误分布（检测差但关联强），且颜色分配可作为涌现的重识别信号，为MOT提供了全新范式。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) is conventionally decomposed into detection followed by association, with object identity maintained as external state: track buffers, motion models, and appearance embeddings. We ask whether a video generator can maintain that state in pixels. We fine-tune a 22B text-to-video diffusion model (LTX-2.3) with a lightweight in-context LoRA to translate an RGB clip into an ID-map clip, a video in which every person is painted a flat, distinct color that persists over time: same color, same identity. Long videos are generated as chained windows, where each window is conditioned on the cleaned tail of the previous one. A brief continuation fine-tune teaches the model to extend a given coloring, after which identity flows through the chain with no tracker, no motion model, and no re-identification module. On the DanceTrack test server, our system, to our knowledge the first generative tracker evaluated there and the only entry with no detector and no tracking stack, reaches 40.3 HOTA. This is well below today's specialist state of the art (>=70 HOTA), but with a unique, inverted error profile: its association score (AssA 44.1) exceeds every tracker of the original benchmark suite while detection remains the sole deficit. Controlled comparisons show the mechanism matters: the same generated windows linked by classical post-hoc association score 2x worse (18.2 HOTA), and frame-to-frame IoU association fragments tracks that the generator's colors keep whole. On 383 mined occlusion events, the generator re-acquires identities after gaps at a 42% conditional rate where appearance-embedding baselines score zero, including gaps longer than its temporal context, evidence that the generator's color assignment functions as an emergent re-identification signal. We release code, checkpoints, and the full pre-registered experimental log.

</details>


### [104] [Learning Emotion from Motion: Kinetic Multi-Stream Skeleton Modeling with Metadata-Conditioned Weak Label Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17121)
*Sosuke Suzuki,Yijin Wei,Koichiro Kamide,Ran Dong,Haoran Xie,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多分支骨架情绪识别框架，通过融合旋转分支、运动多流分支和弱标签分布学习分支，在DIEM-A任务中将准确率从0.271提升至0.366，Macro-F1从0.252提升至0.353。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨架情绪识别面临挑战：情感表达依赖细微动态和关系线索，且硬标签难以捕捉相关情绪类别间的模糊性。

Method: 提出多分支框架，包含：6D旋转分支、部位感知运动多流分支、元数据条件弱标签分布学习分支。各分支独立训练，推理时通过概率级集成融合。

Result: 10折留表演者交叉验证中，准确率从0.271提升至0.366，Macro-F1从0.252提升至0.353。

Conclusion: 速度和骨骼流、手臂和腿部区域对识别情感身体运动至关重要，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Abstract: Skeleton-based emotion recognition from body motion remains challenging because emotional expressions are often characterized by subtle dynamic and relational motion cues, and hard labels may not fully capture ambiguity among related emotion categories. For the DIEM-A task in the MMAC ACII 2026 Challenge, we propose a multi-branch skeleton-based emotion recognition framework that combines a 6D rotation-based branch, a part-aware kinetic multi-stream branch, and a metadata-conditioned weak label distribution learning (LDL) branch. The branches are trained independently and fused by a probability-level ensemble at inference time. In 10-fold leave-performer-out cross-validation, the proposed framework improves Accuracy from 0.271 to 0.366 and Macro-F1 from 0.252 to 0.353 over the rotation-based baseline. Explainability ablations show that velocity and bone streams, as well as arm and leg regions, provide important cues for recognizing emotional body motion.

</details>


### [105] [Denoising Models Develop Human-Like Perceptual Illusion Representations Across Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17138)
*Gautam Ranka,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现去噪神经网络在内部层中存在对亮度错觉敏感的表征，这些表征与人类感知高度相关，但不会影响最终输出，作者称这种现象为“感知幻影”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅从输出层面（像素、分类等）证明神经网络能复现亮度错觉，但模型内部是否真正表征错觉以及其机制尚不清楚。

Method: 通过分析不同去噪模型的中间层激活，识别对错觉敏感的具体层和通道；用FLODOG心理物理模型验证相关性；通过通道消融提供因果证据。

Result: 去噪目标比架构更重要；内部激活与人类亮度感知模型高度相关（Spearman ρ≥0.70），且随错觉强度单调变化；消融实验证明这些通道显著影响内部信号，但注入生成管道后输出无像素变化。

Conclusion: 首次揭示了去噪视觉模型中存在“感知幻影”——内部表征活跃但无法通过输出评估检测到，类似于语言模型中的内部-输出分离现象。

Abstract: Deep neural networks trained on natural images are shown to produce outputs consistent with human observers for brightness illusions. While this phenomenon has been documented across architectures, all evidence, to date, is measured at the output level: restored pixels, decoded trajectories, or classification decisions. Whether these models actually represent illusions internally, and if so where and how, remains unknown. We show that denoising models develop illusion-sensitive representations at specific internal layers, across varied architectures. Specifically, we identify the layers and channels that discriminate illusory from physically matched control regions. We show that the denoising objective is a more important driver of the effect than the architecture. On domain-appropriate stimuli, these activations track a validated psychophysical model of human brightness perception (FLODOG; Spearman $ρ\geq 0.70$) and scale monotonically with parametric illusion strength. Leveraging these findings, we provide causal evidence via channel ablation showing that illusion-sensitive channels specifically and substantially affect the internal signal. Yet injecting these representations into the generation pipeline produces no measurable pixel shift across all tested architectures; we term such representations perceptual phantoms: active in internal processing yet invisible to any output-based evaluation. While related internal-output dissociations have been characterized in language models, this is the first such characterization for perceptual representations in denoising vision models.

</details>


### [106] [STBridge: Shared-Target Alignment for Bridging Understanding and Generation in UMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17140)
*Ye Wang,Hongjun Wang,Hao Fang,Tongyuan Bai,Zuwei Long,Peixian Chen,Wei Liu,Weibo Gu,Xing Sun,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STBridge框架，通过共享目标对齐策略（先监督微调建立通道，再强化学习优化协调），缩小统一多模态模型中视觉理解与生成之间的语义差距，在图像编辑等任务中验证了该方法能有效提升模型描述与生成结果的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型虽然架构上统一了视觉理解与生成，但两者之间存在语义不一致的"理解-生成对齐鸿沟"：模型可能正确描述目标却生成不一致的编辑结果，表明架构统一不足以保证语义统一。

Method: 提出STBridge共享目标对齐框架，采用"先对齐后优化"策略：首先通过监督微调建立共享目标通道，然后使用序列强化学习进一步细化以目标为中心的协调，将理解与生成通过共同目标状态连接起来。

Result: 在视觉理解、图像生成和图像编辑等多个基准上，STBridge相比初始模型持续提升性能。对齐分析证实了该方法有效缩小了模型描述与生成结果之间的差距。

Conclusion: 共享目标对齐是一种有效的后训练策略，能够弥合统一多模态模型中理解与生成之间的鸿沟，仅靠架构统一不足以实现语义统一。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to integrate visual understanding and generation within a single architecture, but architectural unification alone does not ensure semantic consistency. A model may describe the intended target correctly while generating an inconsistent edit. This exposes an understanding-generation alignment gap: linguistic and visual outputs live in different spaces, yet should be governed by the same target semantics. We study this gap in image editing, where an instruction defines a target state that can be both described and visually realized. Given a source image and an edit instruction, we compare a UMM's target caption with its edited image to test whether the two outputs converge on the same result. Our analysis shows that existing UMMs remain weakly aligned, especially for fine-grained entities, attributes, spatial relations, and local details, indicating that semantic unification is not achieved by architecture alone. To bridge this gap, we propose STBridge, a shared-target alignment framework that connects understanding and generation through a common target state. Here the target caption expresses the desired visual result, while the edited image realizes it visually, replacing separate task-specific paths with a shared information flow from target expression to target realization. STBridge follows an align-then-optimize strategy: supervised fine-tuning first establishes the shared-target channel, and sequential reinforcement learning further refines target-centered coordination. Across visual understanding, image generation, and image editing benchmarks, STBridge consistently improves over the initialization model. Alignment analysis confirms that STBridge narrows the gap between what the model describes and what it generates, demonstrating shared-target alignment as an effective post-training strategy for bridging understanding and generation in UMMs.

</details>


### [107] [Noise-Robust Box-Supervised Infrared Small Target Detection via Physics-Inspired Soft Label Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17148)
*Xizhe Zhang,Fan Shi,Mianzhao Wang,Jiangpeng Zheng,Xu Cheng,Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO方法，通过热斑锚定标签优化从含噪的边界框监督中生成稳定的像素级软标签，用于红外小目标检测，在宽松或偏移框标注下表现出强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测依赖像素级掩码标注，但标注成本高且目标边界模糊、纹理弱，导致标注存在固有不确定性。现有框监督方法难以处理高度污染的框标注。

Method: 提出热斑锚定标签优化（HALO），先在框内基于局部背景统计约束定位辐射锚点，再围绕锚点合成物理锚定高斯（PAG）软标签，将噪声框监督转化为连续像素级软标签，整个过程离线进行。

Result: 在标准紧框下与代表性方法相当；在更接近真实场景的宽松或偏移框下鲁棒性显著提升，且跨骨干网络保持一致。

Conclusion: HALO有效解决了框监督IRSTD中标签污染问题，引入污染感知的运行区间分析有助于理解该类方法的有效边界及信杂比与性能的关系。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) commonly relies on pixel-level mask supervision. Such annotations, however, are costly and inherently uncertain because infrared targets have blurred boundaries and weak textures. We formulate box-supervised IRSTD as a problem distinct from generic box-to-mask segmentation and point-supervised IRSTD. Its central challenge is to construct stable pixel-level soft supervision from highly contaminated boxes. To this end, we propose Hotspot-Anchored Label Optimization (HALO). HALO localizes a radiometric anchor inside each box under local background-statistics constraints, then synthesizes a Physically Anchored Gaussian (PAG) soft label around the anchor. This turns noisy box supervision into continuous, pixel-level soft labels. The entire process is performed offline before training, remains decoupled from the detector backbone, and requires no online label updates. Experiments on public datasets show that HALO is competitive with representative box-supervised methods under standard tight boxes. Under looser or shifted box annotations that better approximate real scenarios, HALO is substantially more robust while remaining consistent across backbones. We further introduce a contamination-aware operating-regime analysis to characterize the effective boundary of this class of methods and reveal how intrinsic signal-to-clutter ratio relates to performance.

</details>


### [108] [VLA-ReID: Video-Level Association for Re-Identification in Multi-Object Tracking with Highly Similar Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17157)
*Yanrong Qin,Xiaoyan Cao,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLA-ReID方法，通过视频级关联建模和帧共同外观抑制技术，解决了多目标跟踪中微小、密集、外观相似目标（如蜂群）的长期身份保持问题，在BEE24数据集上显著提升了跟踪性能并减少了28%的身份切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跟踪器依赖单实例分配训练的重识别模型，但在推理时多目标跟踪需要全局分配，这种训练-推理不匹配导致外观相似目标易发生身份切换；同时现有方法需要大量额外标注来增强外观区分性。

Method: 提出视频级关联重识别（VLA-ReID），使用聚合的历史轨迹特征作为查询、当前帧所有检测作为候选，直接优化每帧的全局关联；提出帧共同外观估计（FCAE）和共同外观抑制（CAS），无需额外标注即可放大高度相似目标间的区分性差异。

Result: 在BEE24数据集上，HOTA提升1.1，MOTA提升0.3，AssR提升2.6，AssA提升0.7，IDF1提升0.8，身份切换减少28%。

Conclusion: 视频级重识别建模对多目标跟踪中基于外观的关联是有效的，能显著提升密集相似场景下的跟踪性能。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) aims to localize multiple objects in videos while preserving their identities over time. Long-term identity preservation remains difficult when objects are small, densely distributed, and highly similar in appearance, as in bee swarm scenes. Existing trackers rely on re-identification (re-ID) models trained through single-instance assignment (instance-level querying). At inference, however, MOT requires global assignment between multiple trajectories and detections, corresponding to video-level querying. This training-inference mismatch can cause identity switches among visually similar objects. Existing approaches also often require substantial additional annotations to enhance appearance discrimination. We propose Video-Level Association re-ID (VLA-ReID), which reformulates re-ID as video-level association modeling. It uses aggregated historical trajectory features as queries and all current-frame detections as candidates, enabling direct optimization of their global association at each frame. In addition, Frame-Common Appearance Estimation (FCAE) estimates a common appearance direction from current-frame detections, while Common-Appearance Suppression (CAS) removes the corresponding component along this direction from trajectory and detection features. This amplifies discriminative differences among highly similar objects without additional annotations. Experiments on BEE24 show that VLA-ReID improves HOTA by 1.1, MOTA by 0.3, AssR by 2.6, AssA by 0.7, and IDF1 by 0.8 over state-of-the-art trackers, while reducing identity switches by 28%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of video-level re-ID modeling for appearance-based association in MOT.

</details>


### [109] [BanClickThumb: A Multimodal Dataset and Transformer Fusion Benchmarks for Clickbait Detection in Bengali YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17182)
*Md. Ariful Islam,Md Tanvirul Islam,Md. Maruf Hossain Miru,Md Khalid Syfullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BanClickThumb数据集（7,147个孟加拉语YouTube缩略图-标题对），并提出多模态融合模型BanClickFusionFormer，在点击诱饵检测任务上达到0.84的准确率，优于单模态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语视频点击诱饵检测面临多模态公开数据集匮乏的挑战，现有研究难以有效开展。

Method: 构建包含5个内容域、7,147对缩略图-标题的BanClickThumb数据集，采用文本（XLM-RoBERTa）、图像（SwiftFormer）和融合模型（ViT+XLM-RoBERTa中间融合）进行基准测试。

Result: BanClickFusionFormer多模态模型达到0.84准确率，优于文本模型（0.82）和图像模型（0.68）；密集文本、比喻语言和文化特定俚语仍是难点。

Conclusion: 多模态融合对孟加拉语点击诱饵检测有效，为低资源多模态内容分析提供了公开基准。

Abstract: Clickbait, where video titles and thumbnails exaggerate or misrepresent content, reduces user trust, wastes attention, and promotes misinformation on video-sharing platforms. Detecting Bengali clickbait remains challenging because publicly available multimodal datasets are limited. To address this gap, we introduce BanClickThumb, a curated dataset of 7,147 Bengali YouTube thumbnail-title pairs from five content domains, annotated by ten annotators with high agreement (Cohen's Kappa: 0.83-0.93). Using this dataset, we benchmark text-only, image-only, and multimodal approaches. Among unimodal models, BanClickTextFormer (XLM-RoBERTa) achieves 0.82 accuracy, while BanClickImageFormer (SwiftFormer) reaches 0.68. Our proposed multimodal model, BanClickFusionFormer, combines ViT and XLM-RoBERTa through intermediate fusion and achieves the best accuracy of 0.84. Error analysis shows that dense thumbnail text, figurative language, and culturally specific slang remain challenging. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of multimodal fusion for Bengali clickbait detection and provide a publicly available benchmark to support future research on low-resource multimodal content analysis.

</details>


### [110] [Cross-Coordinate Correspondence Pruning for Image-to-Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17200)
*Xin Liu,Rong Qin,Huipeng Lin,Leizhi Shu,Jin Wu,Chi-Man Vong,Liang Lin,Jufeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对I2P配准中存在的点云密度权衡问题，提出CCP剪枝策略和MDPE集成策略，在保持低离群率的同时获取充足内点，注册召回率较现有SOTA提升至少8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无检测I2P配准方法在粗匹配阶段忽视了点云密度的关键影响：稀疏点云内点不足，密集点云离群率高，形成固有的密度权衡，严重限制了配准精度。

Method: 1) 将跨坐标粗对应投影到2D图像坐标系进行空间统一；2) 轻量级剪枝网络从坐标几何和模态特征维度预测内点置信度，过滤粗离群点；3) 多密度点集成策略合并并去重不同密度下的剪枝对应，最大化内点召回率。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，注册召回率较现有最先进方法提升至少8.6%，性能显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提CCP策略有效缓解了点云密度权衡问题，在保证低离群率的同时获取充足内点，显著提升了I2P配准的准确性。

Abstract: Recent detection-free approaches have shown significant efficacy in image-to-point cloud (I2P) registration by employing a coarse-to-fine matching pipeline. In the coarse stage, down-sampled image features and voxelized point cloud features are typically fused to establish initial coarse correspondences for subsequent refinement. However, existing methods largely overlook the critical role of point cloud density, which fundamentally dictates the quality of coarse correspondences and the final registration results. Specifically, excessively sparse point clouds lead to an insufficient number of inliers, while overly dense ones often introduce a high outlier ratio. Consequently, this creates an inherent density trade-off, thereby significantly limiting the registration accuracy of current approaches. For mitigating this trade-off, we propose a novel Cross-Coordinate Correspondences Pruning (CCP) strategy to acquire sufficient inliers while ensuring a low outlier ratio. To minimize interference from inter-modal coordinate discrepancies, we first project cross-coordinate coarse correspondences to the 2D image coordinate system for spatial unification. Subsequently, a lightweight pruning network is responsible for predicting the inlier confidences, which are used to filter coarse outliers, from coordinate geometric and modal feature dimensions. To maximize inlier recall, we further design a Multi-Density Point Ensemble (MDPE) strategy that consolidates and deduplicates pruned coarse correspondences across varying point cloud densities. Our method achieves a significant performance improvement, surpassing existing state-of-the-art methods by at least 8.6% in Registration Recall across various benchmarks.

</details>


### [111] [Induce to Empower: Improving Lightweight Baselines via Foundation Model Induction for Generalized Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17208)
*Shivanshu Agnihotri,Snehashis Majhi,Deepak Ranjan Nayak,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Debesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Lite-Polyp Inductor (Lite-Pi)，一种轻量级息肉分割基础模型归纳框架，通过生成FM原型表示、对齐先验知识及Transformer融合，显著提升轻量级模型的分割性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型（如DINOv2、SAM）泛化能力强但计算成本高且缺乏标注数据，轻量模型（如U-Net）计算高效但表征能力有限难以跨数据集泛化，两者之间存在明显差距。

Method: 1) 生成FM特定的原型表示，通过重建监督与基础模型先验进行语义对齐；2) 引入基于Transformer的融合机制，突出息肉相关表征（含显著边界信息），同时保留互补语义线索。

Result: 在五个息肉分割基准数据集上，Lite-Pi显著提升了轻量级基线的性能，实现了优越的泛化性能且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: Lite-Pi为息肉分割提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案，缩小了基础模型与轻量模型之间的差距，兼具泛化能力与实时部署可行性。

Abstract: Automated polyp segmentation in colonoscopy continues to pose challenges due to substantial appearance variations and indistinct polyp boundaries. Although emerging foundation models (FMs) such as DINOv2, SAM, and OneFormer, demonstrate remarkable generalization capabilities, their direct transfer to the polyp segmentation task and deployment in real-time clinical settings are difficult due to lack of large-scale labeled data and high computational demands. In addition, adopting multiple FMs together raises concerns, even though they encode complementary semantic and structural information. While lightweight models, including U-Net, PraNet and U-Net++, are computationally efficient, they often struggle to generalize across datasets due to limited representational capacity. To address this gap, we propose Lite-Polyp Inductor (Lite-Pi), a novel foundation model induction framework that significantly enhances lightweight polyp segmentation baselines. Our proposed framework generates FM-specific prototype representations and aligns them semantically with the corresponding foundation model priors through reconstruction-based supervision. Subsequently, transformer-based fusion is introduced to highlight the polyp relevant representations, including salient boundary information, while preserving complementary semantic cues. Extensive experiments across five polyp segmentation benchmark datasets demonstrate that Lite-π significantly improves lightweight baselines, achieving superior generalization performance with minimal computational overhead and thereby, offering a practical solution for generalized polyp segmentation. Our code is available at GitHub. https://github.com/lostinrepo/Lite-Pi

</details>


### [112] [Semantic Context Matters: Analysis of Color Names Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17221)
*Adilet Yerkin,Elnara Kadyrgali,Malika Ziyada,Nuray Toganas,Muragul Muratbekova,Ayan Igali,Aruzhan Sabitkyzy,Pakizar Shamoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过将化妆品、Crayola蜡笔和汽车颜色数据集映射到COLIBRI颜色模型的86个模糊类别中，分析了语境对颜色命名的影响。结果表明，不同语境对颜色空间的覆盖存在显著差异：Crayola覆盖最广且最均衡（50个类别），化妆品集中在暖色调区域（48个类别），汽车颜色偏向蓝色和无彩色区域（40个类别）。研究强调，颜色命名不能仅由数值相似性解释，语义语境起着重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有颜色命名研究多基于物理颜色值，忽略了语义语境对颜色命名的影响，因此需要探索不同语境下颜色命名的差异。

Method: 将化妆品、Crayola蜡笔和汽车颜色数据集映射到COLIBRI颜色模型的86个模糊颜色类别中，通过类别覆盖率、香农熵和最大提升度分析语境变化。

Result: 化妆品覆盖48个类别，Crayola覆盖50个，汽车颜色覆盖40个；Crayola最均衡，化妆品集中在暖色调，汽车颜色偏向蓝色和无彩色区域。

Conclusion: 颜色命名不能仅由数值颜色相似性解释，语义语境在人类颜色理解中发挥重要作用；该框架可用于设计分析、产品搜索、推荐系统和人机交互等领域。

Abstract: Color naming is influenced not only by physical color values but also by the semantic context in which colors are used. This paper investigates context-dependent color naming by mapping color-name datasets from Cosmetics, Crayola, and Car-color vocabularies onto the 86 fuzzy color categories of the COLIBRI color model. Contextual variation is analyzed using category coverage, Shannon entropy, and maximum lift. The results show that the three contexts occupy the COLIBRI color space differently: Cosmetics covers 48 of 86 fuzzy categories, Crayola covers 50, and Car colors cover 40. The results demonstrated that Crayola provides the broadest and most balanced use of the fuzzy color space, Cosmetics is mainly concentrated around warm-tone regions, and Car colors are more specialized around blue and achromatic regions. These findings show that color naming cannot be fully explained by numerical color similarity alone and that semantic context plays an important role in human color interpretation. The proposed framework supports the development of context-aware color models for design analytics, product search, recommendation systems, and human-centered artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [113] [VecFontLLM: Anchor-Guided Direct Synthesis of Chinese Vector Fonts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17251)
*Hao Yuan,Yuxuan Luo,Xing Chen,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VecFontLLM，一种基于锚点引导的多模态大语言模型，用于直接进行中文矢量字体的少样本合成。核心思路是通过锚点（anchor）而非标准矢量序列来生成矢量字形：先预测并优化锚点骨架确定粗略布局，再补全贝塞尔控制点恢复局部曲率和风格。测试时采用置信度引导的生成链，从最高置信度候选继续合成，提升复杂字形的稳定性。实验表明该方法在矢量域直接实现了高质量少样本合成，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文矢量字体包含复杂的组件结构、锚点布局和贝塞尔曲线细节，这些在不同尺度上工作，但标准矢量序列将它们写成一个长序列，使得矢量字体合成任务具有挑战性。现有直接矢量生成器常在复杂字符上失败，而光栅域方法必须事后对合成的字形图像进行矢量化。

Method: 提出VecFontLLM，一种锚点引导的多模态大语言模型。首先预测并优化锚点骨架以固定组件和轮廓的粗略布局，然后补全贝塞尔控制点以恢复局部曲率和风格。测试时使用置信度引导的生成链，采样多个组件候选并从最高置信度候选继续合成。

Result: 在多个中文字体数据集上的实验表明，该方法在矢量域直接合成复杂中文矢量字形方面显著优于现有矢量字体合成方法，渲染质量与光栅域基线相当，且矢量命令分布接近真实字体。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了无需光栅生成或矢量化的高质量复杂中文矢量字形的少样本直接合成，为矢量字体生成领域提供了新思路。

Abstract: Direct generation of Chinese vector fonts is a challenging and ongoing problem. A Chinese vector glyph contains complex component structure, anchor layout, and Bézier curve details, which work at different scales, but a standard vector sequence writes them together in one long sequence, making the task of vector font synthesis challenging. Existing direct vector generators often fail on complex characters, while raster-domain methods must vectorize the synthesized glyph images afterward. To address the above-mentioned problem, this paper proposes VecFontLLM, an anchor-guided multimodal large language model for direct few-shot synthesis of Chinese vector fonts. Our key idea is to generate vector glyphs through anchors rather than a standard vector sequence. Specifically, the proposed VecFontLLM first predicts and refines an anchor scaffold that fixes the coarse layout of components and contours, and then completes Bézier control points to recover local curvature and style. At test time, a confidence-guided generation chain samples multiple component candidates and continues synthesis from the highest-confidence one, improving stability for complex glyphs. This work demonstrates, for the first time, high-quality few-shot synthesis of complex Chinese vector glyphs directly in the vector domain, without raster generation or vectorization. Experiments on several Chinese font datasets show substantial improvements over existing vector font synthesis methods, competitive glyph rendering quality against raster-domain baselines, and vector command distributions close to real fonts.

</details>


### [114] [PACE: Polar Axis-Conditioned Estimation for PairUAV Relative Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17268)
*Ze Rong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Polar Axis-Conditioned Estimation (PACE)方法，通过为航向和距离分配不同的解码器接口，解决了无人机相对定位中两类输出特征不一致的问题，在官方测试集上取得0.001874的最终分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将航向和距离作为同质坐标处理，使用相同的解码器和优化状态，但研究表明二者在解码器深度偏好、最佳检查点一致性（80.8%不一致）及误差分布（距离存在高误差尾部）上存在显著差异，亟需定制化的特征提取方案。

Method: 保留共享的Reloc3r-style对表示，为航向分配中/晚期关系证据，为距离分配直接晚期度量路径，实现轴条件化的独立读取接口。

Result: 官方隐藏测试上，原始预测器得分为0.002460，互补PAAER预测器得分为0.002514（角度误差略低），确定性挑战封装后最终得分为0.001874。

Conclusion: 通过轴条件化读取机制有效区分航向和距离的不同特征需求，提升了无人机相对定位精度。

Abstract: PairUAV relative localization maps two UAV images to a polar navigation command. Although heading and range share the same pairwise pose context, treating them as homogeneous coordinates forces both outputs to use the same decoder evidence and optimization state. Controlled readout probes reveal a different structure: the two axes favor different decoder-depth combinations, their best checkpoints disagree on 80.8% of a validation trajectory, and range errors exhibit a distinct high-error tail. We introduce method, Polar Axis-Conditioned Estimation, which retains a shared Reloc3r-style pair representation while assigning axis-specific readout interfaces. Heading uses mid/late relational evidence, whereas range remains attached to a direct late metric path. On the official hidden test, the strongest released raw predictor scores 0.002460; the complementary PAAER predictor scores 0.002514 with a slightly lower angle error. Deterministic challenge packaging, reported separately from learned estimation, yields the final score of 0.001874. Code, checkpoints, predictions, and reconstruction tools are available at https://github.com/zerong7777-boop/PairUAV-PACE.

</details>


### [115] [MIS-HCC: Hierarchical Channel Clustering for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17329)
*Bo Zhao,Haoran Yu,Lifei Liu,Zongcheng Chu,Yining Liu,Chang Liu,Szu-Yu Chen,Zequn Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MIS-HCC的分层聚类压缩方法，用于在资源受限的医疗平台上高效部署医学图像分割模型。该方法通过Wasserstein距离衡量通道间相似性，并利用层次聚类对通道进行分组融合，从而在保持高精度的同时实现模型压缩。在三个医学图像数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确性和压缩效率上均优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法大多忽视深度神经网络内部结构的固有联系和相似性，导致压缩后的模型难以有效保留预训练网络的基本特征，无法在资源受限的医疗平台上高效部署。

Method: 提出MIS-HCC（医学图像分割模型层次聚类压缩方法），利用Wasserstein距离计算预训练网络层内通道间的相似性矩阵，通过层次聚类对通道进行分组，然后对每个聚类内的通道进行参数融合，生成压缩网络。

Result: 在三个医学图像数据集上的实验结果表明，MIS-HCC在准确性和压缩效率两方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MIS-HCC为在资源受限医疗平台上部署医学图像分割模型提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models require both high accuracy and lightweight design to accommodate real-world medical applications. The deployment of these models on resource-limited medical platforms remains a significant challenge due to their high computational and parameter requirements. Existing pruning methods for model compression mostly overlook the intrinsic connections and similarity between the internal structures of complex deep neural networks. As a result, compressed models may not effectively retain the basic features of the pretrained network. To solve this problem, we propose a hierarchical clustering compression method for medical image segmentation models (MIS-HCC). This approach employs hierarchical clustering to partition channels and fuse their parameters efficiently. Specifically, it leverages the Wasserstein distance to represent similarity of channels within layers of pre-trained network, forming a similarity matrix that guides the clustering process. Channels within each cluster are then fused to produce a compressed network. Experimental results on three medical image datasets application demonstrate that MIS-HCC outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and compression efficiency, offering an effective solution for deploying medical image segmentation models on resource-limited medical platforms.

</details>


### [116] [Orthogonal Knowledge Refreshing for Domain-Incremental Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17340)
*Aoting Zhang,Dongbao Yang,Chang Liu,Xiaopeng Hong,Can Ma,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出正交知识刷新框架(OKR)，通过构建独立域子空间和梯度正交刷新策略，实现无冲突的域增量目标检测，在Pascal VOC和BDD100K上分别提升+5.6%和+6.5% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域增量目标检测面临旧知识被覆盖、域间干扰和性能下降的问题，现有参数高效微调方法存在这些不足。

Method: 1) 为每个域构建独立的低秩分支子空间；2) 梯度正交刷新策略，将新域梯度投影到历史子空间正交补上；3) 拓扑一致性约束，对齐新旧域语义结构。

Result: 在Pascal VOC系列上比最优无范例方法提升+5.6% mAP，在BDD100K系列上提升+6.5% mAP。

Conclusion: OKR能有效解决域增量目标检测中的知识干扰与遗忘问题，是一种简单高效的框架。

Abstract: Domain-incremental object detection (DIOD) requires models to continually adapt to new domains while preserving prior knowledge. Recently, parameter-efficient fine-tuning offers a promising avenue, wherein a pre-trained model is frozen and a small number of learnable parameters are injected for downstream tasks. However, these methods risk overwriting critical past knowledge, triggering inter-domain interference and performance degradation. To address this challenge, we propose Orthogonal Knowledge Refreshing (OKR), a simple yet effective framework for DIOD. OKR incrementally constructs independent domain-specific subspaces via dedicated low-rank branches for each domain, which are seamlessly fused for a holistic decision, enabling conflict-free capacity expansion without domain selection during inference. To minimize knowledge interference during fusion, we present a gradient-based orthogonal refreshing strategy that projects gradient updates of new domains onto the orthogonal complement of the fused historical subspace, supporting continual adaptation without forgetting. Moreover, to mitigate semantic fragmentation across domains, we enforce topology-aware consistency, aligning the semantic structures of old and new domains. Extensive experiments validate the superiority of OKR, outperforming the best exemplar-free method by significant margins of +5.6% and +6.5% mAP on the Pascal VOC and BDD100K series, respectively.

</details>


### [117] [Understanding From Human Perspective: A Multi-agent System for Interactive Egocentric Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17341)
*Rongjun Ge,Dongyang Wang,Heng Zhu,Zhirui Li,Yang Chen,Yuting He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoMed-Agent多智能体系统，通过目标确认工作流和定位引导传播工作流，解决智能眼镜辅助医疗图像分割中的语义歧义和视觉变化问题，在Dice指标上达到71.34%，远超基线方法11.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能眼镜辅助的医学图像回顾场景中，交互式自我中心医学图像分割面临两大挑战：从用户自我中心视角出发的指令存在语义歧义，难以确认用户意图目标；自我中心视频的视觉变异性导致分割结果在帧间跳跃。现有方法无法有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出EgoMed-Agent多智能体系统，包含两个核心工作流：(1) 目标确认工作流，通过可靠性评分将指令与候选目标进行对齐，对可靠目标进行确认，否则请求用户澄清；(2) 定位引导传播工作流，将掩码传播与逐帧目标定位相结合，当两者出现分歧时使用定位结果纠正传播掩码，确保分割在自我中心视频中始终跟随目标。

Result: EgoMed-Agent在平均Dice指标上达到71.34%，远优于最佳文本提示基线方法的11.70%。

Conclusion: EgoMed-Agent通过多智能体协作，有效解决了自我中心视角下的语义歧义和视觉变化问题，在智能眼镜辅助医疗图像分割任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Interactive egocentric medical image segmentation (IEMIS) plays an important role in smart-glasses-assisted medical image review, segmenting the medical targets a clinician refers to from their egocentric view. Once it succeeds, the object-level visual evidence it provides strengthens the review and underpins fine-grained analysis and clinical decision-making. However, the instruction and the video both come from the user's egocentric perspective, which poses two challenges. (1) Semantic ambiguity leaves the model unable to confirm the user-intended target. (2) Visual variability makes the segmentation jump from frame to frame. In this paper, we propose EgoMed-Agent, a multi-agent system that understands the target from the human perspective through two workflows. (1) The \textit{Target Confirmation Workflow} grounds the instruction against candidate targets with a reliability score, confirming the target when the grounding is reliable and asking the user to clarify when it is not, thereby confirming the segmentation target. (2) The \textit{Localization-Guided Propagation Workflow} couples mask propagation with per-frame target localization, using the localized target to correct the propagated mask whenever the two diverge, so the segmentation stays on the target across the egocentric video. Extensive experiments show that EgoMed-Agent reaches 71.34\% average Dice, far above the best text-prompted baseline (11.70\%). Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/wdyyyyyy/EgoMed-Agent}{our project page}.

</details>


### [118] [STAR: Skeletal Token Alignment and Rearrangement for Interaction Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17342)
*Yuhang Wen,Mengyuan Liu,Zixuan Tang,Junsong Yuan,Sirui Li,Beichen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR（骨架令牌对齐与重排）方法，通过骨架与RGB视频表征在共享隐空间中对齐，实现仅用骨架数据即可进行人-机与人-人交互识别，性能超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于骨架序列的交互识别方法面临两大挑战：1) 难以从骨架数据中学习并有效利用交互线索；2) 骨架数据缺乏视觉信息。为此，作者希望设计一种方法，在训练时利用RGB视频丰富骨架表征，推理时仅依赖骨架数据，兼顾效率与性能。

Method: STAR包含三个关键组件：1) 骨架编码器：通过实体重排(ER)和交互时空令牌(ISTs)捕获细粒度依赖关系；2) 视觉交互编码：采用关注交互(FoI)策略聚焦RGB视频中与交互相关的时空区域；3) 对比学习对齐：将骨架与RGB表征在共享隐空间中对齐，并通过精炼头优化预测。训练时同时使用骨架和RGB数据，推理时仅使用骨架。

Result: 在Chico、HARPER、NTU Mutual 11和26四个数据集上，STAR均取得了优于现有最先进方法的性能。

Conclusion: STAR通过跨模态对齐策略，成功将视觉信息融入骨架表征，使得推理时仅使用骨架数据即可获得视觉增强的交互识别能力，为人机/人人交互识别提供了高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding physical human-robot and human-human interactions is a challenging yet emerging topic in 3D vision. While most existing methods rely on skeleton sequences--effective in low-light and privacy-sensitive environment--they face two major challenges: 1) learning and effectively exploiting interaction cues from skeletal data, and 2) compensating for the lack of visual information absent in skeletons alone. To address these challenges, we propose skeletal token alignment and rearrangement (STAR) for human-robot and human-human interaction recognition. It learns interaction-specific skeleton features and enriches them using visual cues by aligning skeleton and RGB video representations in a shared latent space. Specifically, STAR consists of three key components. First, we design a skeleton encoder that captures fine-grained interdependencies using Entity Rearrangement (ER) and Interactive Spatiotemporal Tokens (ISTs). Second, we present Visual Interaction Encoding that introduces a Focus on Interactions (FoI) strategy to attend to spatiotemporal regions relevant to interactions in RGB videos. Finally, these representations are aligned via a contrastive learning objective, with a refinement head further refines predictions. During training, STAR leverages both skeleton and RGB video data to learn robust, discriminative interaction representations. At inference time, it operates on skeletons alone, retaining visual-informed benefits while preserving skeleton-only efficiency. Extensive experiments on Chico, HARPER, NTU Mutual 11 and 26 datasets consistently validate our approach by demonstrating superior performance over state-of-the-art methods. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Necolizer/STAR.

</details>


### [119] [SkyVLaM: Multimodal Large Language Model for UAV Video Understanding in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17386)
*Kaiwen Jing,Ruixu Jia,Bingyao Li,Ruizhe Ou,Ming Wu,Chuang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SkyVLaM，一种用于无人机视频理解的多模态大语言模型，通过构建稀疏与密集令牌实现语言条件分割，并构建了包含101个视频、33.6K帧和153万像素级实例的SkyVid数据集，实验表明该方法在无人机场景下有效分配视觉令牌预算并提升了语言条件视频分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视频中目标小、视觉模糊且视角动态变化，现有语言条件分割方法难以处理这些挑战。

Method: 提出SkyVLaM，包含：1）通过时序基础感知器从视频块级表示构建稀疏令牌；2）正则化稀疏基础以鼓励互补时序线索；3）自适应选择时序一致的密集段进行高分辨率检查；4）将稀疏和密集令牌联合输入大语言模型进行查询条件分割。同时构建SkyVid数据集（含SkyVid-VGCG和SkyVid-RVOS）。

Result: SkyVLaM能更有效分配视觉令牌预算，在无人机语言条件视频分割场景中表现优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的稀疏-密集令牌联合处理策略有效提升了无人机视频中语言条件分割的性能，验证了多模态大语言模型在该任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly improved remote sensing (RS) multimodal understanding. Language-conditioned segmentation is crucial for fine-grained target understanding in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) videos. However, this task remains challenging due to the prevalence of small, visually ambiguous targets and dynamic aerial perspectives. In this paper, we propose SkyVLaM, a multimodal large language model for UAV video understanding. SkyVLaM constructs sparse tokens directly from patch-level video representations through a temporal basis perceiver, regularizes the sparse basis to encourage complementary temporal cues, and adaptively selects a temporally coherent dense segment for high-resolution inspection. The resulting sparse and dense tokens are jointly processed by a large language model for query-conditioned segmentation. We further build SkyVid, consisting of SkyVid-VGCG and SkyVid-RVOS for video grounded conversation generation and referring video object segmentation, respectively. SkyVid contains 101 videos, 33.6K frames, and 1.53M pixel-level object instances. Experiments show that SkyVLaM provides a more effective allocation of the visual token budget and improves language-conditioned video segmentation in UAV scenarios.

</details>


### [120] [Automating Visual Recognition of Leprosy in Wild Chimpanzees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17395)
*Katie I. Murray,Anna C. Bowland,Marina Ramon,Elena Bersacola,Aissa Regalla,Manmohan D. Sharma,Markus Mueller,Majid Mirmehdi,Dave Hodgson,Kimberley J. Hockings,Tilo Burghardt,Otto Brookes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于野生动物麻风病检测的深度学习管道，并贡献PanLep300数据集。研究发现，简单的图像级预测聚合方法优于复杂的时序模型，符合麻风病静态皮肤表现的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西非野生黑猩猩中已确认存在麻风病，人工筛选大量相机陷阱录像不可行，亟需自动化检测方法。

Method: 构建PanLep300数据集（125,670个标注框，953条轨迹，303个视频），基准测试了2D、2.5D和3D三种分类方法，并设计了生态学驱动的数据划分策略。

Result: 简单的预测聚合方法优于学习型时序模型和端到端视频架构；部分可见个体的轨迹会降低性能，可通过针对性构建和聚合策略改善。

Conclusion: 对于麻风病这类静态皮肤病变，简单的图像级聚合方法足够有效，无需复杂时序模型。

Abstract: Leprosy (Mycobacterium leprae) has been confirmed in wild western chimpanzees (Pan troglodytes verus) in West Africa, presenting as clear and progressive visual symptoms. Manual review of camera-trap footage at landscape scale is infeasible, motivating the need for automated screening. We present the first deep learning pipeline for wildlife leprosy detection and contribute the PanLep300 dataset of 125,670 annotated bounding-box crops across 953 tracks from 303 camera-trap videos with ecologically-motivated splits that withhold whole individuals and camera installations. We benchmark spatial (2D), temporally aggregated (2.5D), and video-based (3D) classification approaches to investigate which approach is best suited to automated leprosy detection in wild apes. We find that simple aggregation of crop-level predictions consistently matches or outperforms both learned temporal models and end-to-end video architectures -- consistent with leprosy's static cutaneous presentation. We further find that performance is suppressed when tracklets contain frames of partially visible individuals -- as commonly occurs at the start and end of a track -- and demonstrate that this can be addressed through targeted construction and aggregation strategies.

</details>


### [121] [The PanAf-SBR Dataset: Social Behaviour Recognition for Wild Great Apes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17399)
*Maciej Braszczok,Otto Brookes,Xiaoxuan Ma,Federico Rossano,Yixin Zhu,Mimi Arandjelovic,Hjalmar Kühl,Majid Mirmehdi,Tilo Burghardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍PanAf-SBR，首个野生类人猿相机陷阱社交行为数据集，包含81,096个标注，并基于AlphaChimp架构建立细粒度社交行为识别基准，发现跨数据集预训练对特定类别有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野生类人猿种群行为变化（如社会结构瓦解）是种群衰退的早期指标，但现有数据集在野生环境下细粒度社交行为标注方面存在空白。

Method: 构建PanAf-SBR数据集（扩展PanAf500，新增100个视频、36,063帧、81,096个标注），采用AlphaChimp架构建立基准，进行跨数据集迁移学习实验，并通过分割掩码反转方法分析背景影响。

Result: 建立了野生类人猿相机陷阱视频中细粒度社交行为识别的首个基准；跨数据集预训练对特定类别有益而非普遍有益；背景上下文对识别性能有影响。

Conclusion: PanAf-SBR填补了野生类人猿社交行为自动识别数据集的空白，为保护监测提供技术支撑，并揭示了迁移学习与背景信息的作用。

Abstract: Behavioural shifts in wild great ape populations, particularly the breakdown of social structures, can serve as an early indicator of population decline. Automating the detection of behaviours indicative of these shifts is therefore a critical task for conservation. Several valuable datasets have recently been introduced for the automated recognition of great ape behaviour, yet few include fine-grained social behaviour annotations, and those that do are captured either in captive settings or via aerial platforms such as UAVs. We address this gap by introducing PanAf-SBR, the first wild great ape camera trap dataset annotated with social behaviours. PanAf-SBR extends PanAf500 with 100 additional videos covering 36,063 frames. These come with 81,096 annotations including bounding boxes, segmentation masks, intra-video identities, and seven social behaviour classes defined under the action giver and receiver convention of ChimpACT. We use this data together with the AlphaChimp architecture to establish the first benchmarks for fine-grained social behaviour recognition in wild great apes from camera trap footage. We further conduct bidirectional transfer learning experiments between PanAf-SBR and the captive ChimpACT dataset, finding that cross-dataset pre-training is highly beneficial for specific classes rather than of uniform benefit. Finally, we examine the role of background context by inverting the segmentation masks to suppress non-ape pixels.

</details>


### [122] [Does Super-Resolution Preserve Defect Evidence? A Low-False-Call Benchmark for Semiconductor Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17401)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 超分辨率技术虽能使检测图像更清晰，但可能丢失缺陷检测所需的关键证据。本文通过分离重建与检测的基准测试发现：学习型超分辨率模型结构相似性最高，但缺陷像素检测能力反而不如双三次插值；直接训练的DeepLabV3检测器在所有测试中均满足可行性标准，而联合模型校准成功却无法保证迁移效果。结论：超分辨率用于检测应用时，应以任务证据保留和操作点迁移性为评判标准，而非仅看重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分辨率技术常用于提升检测图像清晰度，但可能导致缺陷检测所需的原始证据丢失，现有评估方法未能有效区分重建质量与检测实用性之间的差异。

Method: 设计分离重建与检测的基准测试，包含十轮端到端重复实验，使用独立生成的线条/空间和接触孔图像，结合模型训练、校准、干净对照组、弱缺陷和保留的缺陷形态。所有重建图像由同一局部残差检测器评分，同时设置直接检测器和联合训练检测器作为对比轨道。

Result: 学习型超分辨率模型（两种）虽取得最高结构相似性，但在每轮重复实验中检测到的缺陷像素均少于双三次插值。直接DeepLabV3检测器在十次重复中均满足可行性标准，而联合模型DPU-WaferSR虽通过独立干净校准，但十次重复均超出保留限制。所有可行方法对弱缺陷的召回率接近零。

Conclusion: 超分辨率在检测应用中不应仅以重建质量评判，而应以任务证据保留程度和操作点迁移性作为核心评估标准。

Abstract: Super-resolution can make inspection images appear sharper without preserving the evidence needed to detect a defect. We study this failure mode with a benchmark that separates reconstruction from detection and evaluates both at a predeclared low false-positive rate. Ten end-to-end repetitions combine independently generated line/space and contact-hole images with model training, calibration, clean controls, weak defects, and a held-out defect morphology. Every reconstruction is scored by the same local residual detector, while direct and jointly trained detectors form a separate comparison track. Reconstruction fidelity and inspection utility diverge: the two learned reconstruction models attain the highest structural similarity yet detect fewer defect pixels than bicubic interpolation in every paired repetition. A direct DeepLabV3 detector reaches $0.1984\pm0.0385$ recall at $0.000174\pm0.000084$ false-positive rate and satisfies the held-out feasibility criterion in all ten repetitions. An illustrative joint model, DPU-WaferSR, passes independent clean calibration but exceeds the held-out limit in all ten repetitions, demonstrating that calibration success does not guarantee transfer. Weak-defect recall remains near zero for every feasible method. Applying the unchanged policies to 4,591 public Carinthia-S masks further reveals large method-dependent shifts on real SEM texture. These results support a simple conclusion: super-resolution for inspection should be judged by preserved task evidence and operating-point transfer, not reconstruction quality alone.

</details>


### [123] [TimeLens2: Generalist Video Temporal Grounding with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17423)
*Yuhan Zhu,Changlian Ma,Xiangyu Zeng,Xinhao Li,Zhiqiu Zhang,Songze Li,Jun Zhang,Tianxiang Jiang,Yuandong Yang,Ziang Yan,Zikang Wang,Xinyu Chen,Haoran Chen,Shaowei Zhang,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeLens2提出了面向视频时间定位的区间集监督与优化框架，通过构建高质量多跨度标注数据集93K和Wasserstein时间奖励函数，在7个基准测试中超越现有模型，2B/4B/8B版本相比基础模型分别提升14.2/13.0/18.1 mIoU点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态大模型能描述视频内容但难以定位关键证据出现的时间位置；已有训练策略与集合级任务不匹配，如长视频标签依赖脆弱的一遍标注，强化学习奖励无法区分非重叠预测或需要脆弱的片段匹配。

Method: TimeLens2将时间证据视为区间集进行全流程监督与优化。构建TimeLens2-93K数据集：通过字幕派生提案、独立定位、跨智能体共识、语义验证和边界精炼生成可靠的多跨度标注。提出时间Wasserstein奖励：计算合并区间支持上均匀分布间的精确一维W₁距离，提供稠密、无需匹配的反馈，处理不等基数和等效碎片化问题；结合时间IoU提供精确重叠反馈。

Result: 在7个基准测试中，TimeLens2-2B在所有基准上超越所有同规模基线；4B和8B版本达到最优性能，超越高达397B参数的开源模型。2B/4B/8B相对Qwen3-VL基础模型分别提升14.2/13.0/18.1 mIoU点。

Conclusion: 将时间证据建模为区间集进行端到端监督与优化，结合高质量标注数据和专门设计的Wasserstein奖励函数，能有效解决通用视频时间定位问题，显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can describe what happens in a video, but rarely identify when the supporting evidence occurs. We study generalist video temporal grounding, in which one model predicts a variable-cardinality set of evidence intervals across video lengths, domains, query forms, and viewpoints. Existing training strategies are misaligned with this set-valued task: long-video labels often rely on brittle one-pass annotation, while reinforcement-learning rewards either fail to distinguish non-overlapping predictions or require fragile segment matching. TimeLens2 treats temporal evidence as an interval set throughout supervision and optimization. TimeLens2-93K constructs reliable multi-span supervision through caption-derived proposals, independent localization, cross-agent consensus, semantic verification, and boundary refinement. Our temporal Wasserstein reward computes exact one-dimensional \(W_1\) between uniform distributions over merged interval supports, providing dense, matching-free feedback under unequal cardinalities and equivalent fragmentation; temporal IoU complements it with precise-overlap feedback. Across seven benchmarks, TimeLens2-2B outperforms all size-matched baselines on every benchmark, while the 4B and 8B variants achieve state-of-the-art performance, surpassing open-source models with up to 397B parameters. The 2B, 4B, and 8B variants improve over their Qwen3-VL backbones by 14.2, 13.0, and 18.1 mIoU points, respectively.

</details>


### [124] [An Explainable FFT-Based Spatial-Frequency Fusion Framework for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17441)
*Pamela Kirui,Cho Hyuk,Qingzhong Liu,Haodi Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于FFT的多尺度交叉注意力框架MSCA-FFT，用于图像级深度伪造检测，结合Xception空间分支和FFT频域分支，通过交叉注意力融合，在实验中优于基于DCT的最新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造生成技术引发了对数字媒体真实性、虚假信息、身份欺诈和公众信任的担忧。现有研究表明结合空间与频域特征比单独使用效果更好，本文旨在提出更优的融合检测框架。

Method: 采用部分微调的Xception网络作为空间分支，FFT频域分支处理对数缩放幅度谱（通过浅卷积层，避免DCT框架中的逆频域重建），两者经Transformer编码器精炼后通过交叉注意力融合，送入MLP分类器进行真/假预测。

Result: MSCA-FFT在性能上持续优于基于DCT的最新空间-频率融合方法及对比基线模型。消融实验表明FFT频域分支在与空间特征融合时提供了互补的频谱线索。Grad-CAM/LIME解释显示模型关注眼部、口部、鼻子和面部边界等操纵敏感区域。

Conclusion: FFT-based多尺度交叉注意力框架在深度伪造检测中有效且可解释性强，为频域特征融合提供了优于DCT的新思路。

Abstract: Deepfake generation has raised growing concerns regarding digital media authenticity, misinformation, identity fraud, and public trust. Recent studies show that combining spatial and frequency features leads to stronger detection results than using independently. This paper presents MSCA-FFT, a Fast Fourier Transform (FFT)-based multi-scale cross-attention framework for image-level deepfake detection. The model combines a partially fine-tuned Xception spatial branch with an FFT-based frequency branch. The frequency branch processes the log-scaled FFT magnitude spectrum through shallow convolutional layers, avoiding inverse frequency-to-image reconstruction used in DCT-based pipelines. The spatial and frequency representations are refined by transformer encoders, fused through cross-attention, and passed to an MLP classifier for real/fake prediction. Experimental results show that MSCA-FFT achieves consistently higher performance than the DCT-based state-of-the-art spatial-frequency fusion method and the compared baseline models. The ablation study further indicates that the FFT-based frequency branch provides complementary spectral cues when fused with spatial features. In addition, FFT-based frequency analysis and Grad-CAM/LIME explanations show consistent evidence around manipulation-sensitive facial regions, including the eyes, mouth, nose, and facial boundaries.

</details>


### [125] [Bio-SFT: Asymmetric Cortical Guidance and Retinal Adaptation for Robust HDR Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17456)
*Tingyu Cheng,Ting Zhang,Chongyi Li,Zhaoqing Pan,Tiesong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受生物启发的脉冲频率变换器Bio-SFT，用于单图像HDR重建，通过模拟视网膜处理机制有效解决了极端光照和暗区噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张SDR图像恢复HDR辐射图是高度不适定的问题，极端亮度变化和暗区严重量化导致重建困难，常出现视觉伪影和色彩失真。

Method: Bio-SFT包含三个生物启发组件：1) 可学习的Naka-Rushton视网膜自适应前端稳定光照输入；2) 显式的Parvo-Magno分裂引入不对称引导，用高频结构线索调制低频重建；3) 事件驱动的SNN硬门控模块抑制暗区噪声并保留结构细节，通过稀疏先验训练实现高效特征利用。

Result: 在HDRTV1K数据集上，Bio-SFT达到了有竞争力的感知质量，一致提升了HDR-VDP-3和ΔE_ITP指标，同时减少了对称引导流水线中的伪影传播。

Conclusion: 这些轻量级生物启发组件在变换器骨干网络中实现了端到端训练，具有出色的参数效率，有效解决了单图像HDR重建中的关键挑战。

Abstract: Recovering high dynamic range (HDR) radiance from a single standard dynamic range (SDR) image is highly ill-posed. Extreme luminance variation and severe quantization in dark regions make accurate reconstruction challenging, often leading to visual artifacts and color distortions. To address this problem, we propose Bio-SFT, a bio-inspired spiking frequency transformer for single-image HDR reconstruction. Bio-SFT incorporates three biologically motivated components. First, a learnable Naka--Rushton retinal adaptation frontend stabilizes the input under complex lighting conditions. Second, an explicit Parvo--Magno split introduces asymmetric Parvo-to-Magno guidance, allowing high-frequency structural cues to modulate low-frequency reconstruction. Third, an event-driven SNN hard gating module applies all-or-none spiking to suppress dark-region noise while preserving structural details. The module is trained with a sparsity prior to encourage efficient feature utilization. Built for end-to-end training within a transformer backbone, these lightweight components provide strong parameter efficiency. Experiments on HDRTV1K show that Bio-SFT achieves competitive perceptual quality and consistently improves HDR-VDP-3 and $ΔE_{ITP}$ while reducing artifact propagation in symmetric guidance pipelines.

</details>


### [126] [DA-MergeLoRA: Hypernetwork-Based LoRA Merging for Few-Shot Test-Time Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17467)
*Siobhan Reid,Zhixiang Chi,Li Gu,Omid Reza Heidari,Ziqiang Wang,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将LoRA微调与模型合并相结合的新框架，用于少样本测试时域适应（FSTT-DA），通过在CLIP视觉编码器上为每个源域训练独立LoRA模块，并利用元学习训练的超网络生成合并权重来适应目标域，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSTT-DA方法不能有效利用源域特定知识，依赖于浅层批归一化更新、将模型视为黑箱的提示方法或无法捕捉跨域关系的集成策略。

Method: 为每个源域在CLIP视觉编码器上单独微调LoRA模块；提出一个通过元学习训练的超网络，该网络为每个列生成合并因子，以将源域LoRA模块融合为目标域适应的单一表示。

Result: 在多个域适应数据集上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过LoRA保持基础模型的泛化知识并学习域特定特征，结合超网络进行知识合并，有效解决了FSTT-DA中的跨域适应问题。

Abstract: Few-shot Test-Time Domain Adaptation (FSTT-DA) seeks to adapt models to novel domains using only a handful of unlabeled target samples. This setting is more realistic than typical domain adaptation setups, which assume access to target data during source training. However, prior FSTT-DA approaches fail to effectively leverage source domain-specific knowledge, relying on shallow batch normalization updates, prompt-based methods that treat the model as a black box, or ensembling strategies that do not capture cross-domain relationships. To address these limitations, we introduce a new FSTT-DA framework that integrates LoRA fine-tuning with model merging. In our approach, separate LoRA modules are fine-tuned on CLIP's vision encoder for each source domain. Since LoRA modifies only a small fraction of the model's parameters, it retains the base model's generalized knowledge while internally learning domain-specific features. To adapt the learned knowledge to a specific target domain, we propose a hypernetwork trained via meta-learning that generates per-column merging factors to combine LoRA modules. Given a small batch of target images, the hypernetwork produces merging weights that fuse source LoRA modules into a single adapted representation. Our results demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across various domain adaptation datasets. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/nahbois4321/DA-MergeLoRA.

</details>


### [127] [TraversRL: Traversable Pedestrian Pathway Generation With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17479)
*Bin Han,Robert Wolfe,Bill Howe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraversRL是一种通过强化学习从航拍图像中逐步生成行人路径网络的方法，将路径提取建模为旅行者的序贯决策过程，显著提升了网络的连通性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分割的方法生成的行人路径网络常常断裂不连贯，难以用于导航；而人行道和过街区域存在遮挡、隐式定义和复杂连接模式等挑战。

Method: 提出TraversRL模型，采用视觉条件化的迭代生长方式，使用长短方向-距离段动作空间适应复杂模式并跨越遮挡，结合图级和步级奖励平衡整体连通性与局部边缘精度。

Result: 在三种视觉骨干网络和三个交叉路口数据集上，相比最先进的分割基线，缓冲IoU显著提升，连通性指标翻倍以上；全局与局部奖励结合生成更简洁的图并进一步提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 将路径提取建模为旅行者视角的序贯决策过程，并通过强化学习优化最终图质量，能够生成更可靠的行人网络。

Abstract: Automatically generating pedestrian pathways from aerial images requires producing a connected network suitable for routing, not just detecting where sidewalks appear. Sidewalks and crossings, in contrast to roads, may be partially occluded, implicitly defined, and exhibit complex connectivity patterns. Existing segmentation-based approaches focus on labeling pixels to infer segments, but often produce disconnected or fragmentary graphs that are unreliable for navigation. We introduce TraversRL, a vision-conditioned model that iteratively grows a pathway network from an aerial image, simulating a traveler navigating the built environment. TraversRL uses an action space of short and long direction-distance segments designed to adapt to complex patterns and span occlusions, and uses a combination of graph-level and step-wise rewards to balance overall connectivity with precise edge placement. Across three visual backbones and three intersection datasets, TraversRL substantially improves buffered IoU with the ground-truth graph relative to a state-of-the-art segmentation baseline, and more than doubles metrics of connectivity. Moreover, combining global and local rewards produces cleaner graphs with fewer spurious branches while further improving overall performance. These results demonstrate that modeling pathway extraction as a sequential decision process from the perspective of a traveler, while optimizing for final graph quality with reinforcement learning, produces significantly more reliable pedestrian networks.

</details>


### [128] [Generative Transmission: Rethinking Computation, Bandwidth, and Memory in Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17482)
*Xiangyu Chen,Jixiang Luo,Yuankai Fan,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenTrans框架，在超低带宽和弱网络条件下，将视频通信从传统的像素保真传输转向基于生成模型的任务导向传输，通过联合优化带宽、计算和存储，显著提升传输效率、解码效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频编码方法在超低带宽和弱网络条件下难以平衡视觉可用性、传输效率和对不稳定链路的鲁棒性，需要探索基于生成模型的新型视频通信方式。

Method: 基于生成视频压缩（GVC），将视频传输建模为带宽、计算和存储的联合优化问题，采用生成先验、跨片段存储复用、运行时状态复用和弱网络感知传输等技术。

Result: 在超低比特率和弱网络条件下支持有效视频传输，提升了传输效率、解码效率和鲁棒性，同时保持了感知质量。

Conclusion: GenTrans证明了从信号保真向感知效用转变的可行性，为弱网络环境下的视频通信提供了新范式。

Abstract: Under the AI Flow framework, communication is shifting from transmitting fidelity-oriented information flows toward delivering task-oriented and perception-oriented token flows across heterogeneous network resources. Video communication is a fundamental component of modern information networks. However, under ultra-low-bandwidth and weak-network conditions, conventional video coding and transmission methods, which are primarily optimized for pixel-level fidelity, often struggle to balance visual usability, transmission efficiency, and robustness to unstable links. With the rapid advancement of generativemodels, video communication is also moving from precise signal reconstruction toward receiver-side perceptual utility and system-level usability. In this paper, we propose Generative Transmission (GenTrans) for video communication under ultra-low-bandwidth and weak-network conditions. Built upon Generative Video Compression (GVC), GenTrans formulates video transmission as a joint optimization problem involving bandwidth, computation, and memory, rather than treating it merely as a signal coding task. By leveraging generative priors, cross-clip memory reuse, runtime state reuse, and weak-network-aware transport, GenTrans significantly reduces transmission overhead while enabling visually coherent and practically useful reconstruction. Experimental results show that GenTrans supports effective video transmission under ultra-low-bitrate and weak-network conditions, achieving improved transmission efficiency, decoding efficiency, and robustness while preserving perceptual quality.

</details>


### [129] [DecoyFace: Beyond Obfuscation via Controllable and Imperceptible Identity Misdirection for Privacy-Preserving Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17504)
*Zhihan Ren,Lijun He,Xinyao Wang,Xinzhu Fu,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoyFace是一种针对分割式人脸识别的不可感知诱饵防护框架，通过将中间表示分解为重构敏感子空间和互补子空间，在重构敏感子空间注入诱饵身份线索，使未经授权的重构产生看似合理但错误的身份，同时保持识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护人脸识别方法主要抵抗未经授权的图像重构，但产生的特征重构后图像明显退化，暴露了保护机制的存在，容易引发自适应攻击。需要一种既保护隐私又不易被察觉的防护方法。

Method: 将中间表示分解为重构敏感子空间和互补子空间；客户端在重构敏感子空间注入诱饵身份线索，在互补子空间保留真实样本的少量识别相关信息；服务器端通过授权规范化模块压制诱饵主导成分，恢复识别友好的表示。

Result: 在LFW数据集上，U-Net攻击下身份泄露降至2.93%，Flow-Matching攻击下降至0.74%，人脸有效性超过99.78%，同时保持具有竞争力的识别精度。

Conclusion: DecoyFace能够有效抵抗攻击者侧的特征反转攻击和HBC服务器侧的规范化表示重构，在不暴露保护机制存在的前提下实现隐私保护。

Abstract: Split face recognition reduces client-side computation but exposes intermediate features to feature inversion attacks and unauthorized analysis by honest-but-curious (HBC) servers. Existing privacy-preserving face recognition methods mainly aim to resist unauthorized reconstruction, typically producing features whose inversion yields visibly degraded results, which may reveal the existence of protection and motivate adaptive attacks. To address this issue, we propose DecoyFace, an imperceptible decoy-oriented framework that steers unauthorized reconstruction toward a plausible but incorrect identity while preserving recognition utility. The key idea is to decompose the intermediate representation into a reconstruction-sensitive subspace and its complementary subspace. The client injects decoy identity cues into the reconstruction-sensitive subspace, while limited recognition-relevant evidence from the true sample is retained in the complementary subspace. On the server side, an authorized canonicalization module suppresses decoy-dominant components and recovers a recognition-friendly representation. This design addresses both attacker-side inversion from intercepted features and HBC server-side reconstruction from canonicalized representations. Experiments show that DecoyFace preserves competitive recognition accuracy while substantially reducing identity leakage to 2.93% under U-Net attacks and 0.74% under Flow-Matching attacks while yielding visually plausible and imperceptible reconstructions, with over 99.78% face validity on LFW dataset.

</details>


### [130] [Hierarchy-Aware and Anatomy-Guided Learning for Lung Ultrasound Video Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17551)
*Alya Almsouti,Lotfi Mecharbat,Noha Aboukhater,Yousef Alabrach,Siddiq Anwar,Andre Kumar,Ibrahim Almakky,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合层级感知训练与解剖引导学习的深度学习框架，用于超声视频多分类（健康、B线、实变、混合），在公开数据集上达到65.7%的宏F1，并实现可解释的注意力聚焦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺超声（LUS）是评估肺水肿的有效床旁工具，但自动分析受散斑噪声、伪影及操作者依赖性影响而困难重重。现有方法缺乏对临床类别层级关系的利用，且模型注意力难以聚焦于解剖相关区域。

Method: 基于强基线模型，设计层级感知训练策略（利用类别间层级关系），并引入胸膜线掩码监督（anatomy-guided learning），引导模型关注解剖相关区域。在1886个视频（219名患者）上进行五折交叉验证。

Result: 层级感知训练优于扁平分类；掩码引导的注意力监督取得最高平均宏F1为65.7%，且生成更聚焦的注意力图。在外部COVID-BLUeS数据集上迁移实验也表现出竞争性且参数高效。

Conclusion: 将临床结构化目标与解剖引导监督相结合，是实现鲁棒且可解释的肺超声视频分析的实用方法。

Abstract: Lung ultrasound (LUS) is a bedside tool for assessing pulmonary edema in patients at risk due to heart failure or impaired kidney function. However, automated LUS analysis remains challenging because of speckle noise, imaging artifacts, and operator-dependent acquisition variability. In this work, we present a deep learning framework for multi-class LUS video classification that explores two components: hierarchy-aware training, and anatomy-guided learning. Starting from a strong baseline, we introduce hierarchical training strategies and then introduce pleural line mask supervision to guide model attention toward anatomically relevant regions. We study four clinically relevant classes--healthy, B-lines, consolidations, and mixed B-lines with consolidations--using an open-access dataset of 1,886 videos from 219 patients, evaluated with patient-level five-fold cross-validation. Results show that hierarchy-aware training improves pathological separation relative to flat classification, while mask-guided attention supervision achieves the highest mean macro-F1 of 65.7\% and produces more localized attention patterns. Transfer experiments on the external COVID-BLUeS dataset further show competitive and parameter-efficient adaptation while preserving pleural-focused attention behavior. These findings suggest that combining clinically structured objectives with anatomy-guided supervision is a practical approach to robust, interpretable LUS video analysis. Code and model implementations are available at https://github.com/Alya-Almsouti/LUS-video-classification.

</details>


### [131] [FlexiGrad: Adaptive Gradient Modulation for Hierarchical Fine-Grained Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17563)
*Zilu Zhou,Dongliang Chang,Junhan Chen,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexiGrad方法，通过层次感知的梯度调节机制解决分层标签中粗细粒度分类器的梯度冲突问题，无需修改网络结构即可提升多粒度识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度识别任务中分层标签（如目、科、种）的联合优化常因粗细分类器的梯度冲突导致训练不稳定，阻碍模型学习一致的从粗到细的表示。

Method: FlexiGrad是一种无参数的梯度调节方法，在反向传播时移除有害的冲突梯度分量，并通过平滑的层次感知加权函数强化部分一致的共享方向，实现稳定优化。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011、FGVC-Aircraft和Stanford Cars数据集上提升了多粒度分类准确率。

Conclusion: FlexiGrad可无缝集成到现有架构中，无需修改模型结构，有效缓解层次梯度冲突问题。

Abstract: Many fine-grained recognition tasks contain hierarchical labels such as order, family and species. Although this supervision should be beneficial, jointly optimising all levels often leads to unstable training because coarse and fine classifiers impose inconsistent gradients on the shared backbone. This hierarchical gradient conflict prevents the model from learning a coherent coarse-to-fine representation. In this paper, we propose FlexiGrad, a simple and parameter-free method that regulates gradient interactions during backpropagation. FlexiGrad removes only the harmful conflicting component when tasks disagree and reinforces the shared direction when they partially agree through a smooth hierarchy-aware weighting function. This produces stable optimisation and preserves both global structure and fine-grained discriminative cues. FlexiGrad integrates into existing architectures without modification while improves multi-granularity accuracy on CUB-200-2011, FGVC-Aircraft and Stanford Cars. The code will be available at PRIS-CV/FlexiGrad.

</details>


### [132] [Scalable Model-Assisted Multi-Target Estimation in Large Image Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17581)
*Max Hamilton,Jinlin Lai,Daniel Sheldon,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合有偏模型预测和有限人工标注的多目标估计框架，通过自适应采样策略（重要性采样、均匀采样+控制变量、子集比率估计）实现对图像集合中多个类别数量或比例的统计上严谨的无偏估计，实验证明该方法在不同标注预算和目标数量下均能有效工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉模型用于群体级估计时存在预测偏差，缺乏统计保证，而现有蒙特卡洛方法主要解决单标量估计问题，无法高效处理多目标同时估计的场景。

Method: 借鉴调查抽样的采样与估计策略，扩展至多目标估计问题；对比了三种方法：重要性采样、均匀采样+控制变量、子集比率估计，在5个检测/分割数据集（7-80类）上评估。

Result: 重要性采样在中标注预算/较少目标时表现优异；均匀采样+控制变量在估计多目标或极少标注时更优；子集比率估计在所有场景下均具强竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效结合有偏模型预测与有限人工标注，为科学测量提供严谨的统计保证。

Abstract: Computer vision models are increasingly used as measurement tools to estimate population-level quantities from large image collections, but prediction errors introduce bias and the resulting estimates lack statistical guarantees required in scientific applications. Prior work uses a Monte Carlo framework to combine model predictions with ground-truth annotations by sampling some images for humans to label and is able to provide unbiased estimates with controllable accuracy, but primarily addresses single-scalar estimation. We study the more general problem of multi-target estimation, where many quantities (e.g., class counts or proportions) must be estimated simultaneously, and adapt sampling and estimation strategies from survey sampling to this setting. Evaluations on five detection and segmentation datasets with 7-80 classes show that importance sampling excels with moderate annotation budgets or fewer targets, whereas uniform sampling with control variates is superior when estimating many targets or operating with minimal labels. Additionally, a subset-based ratio estimator remains highly competitive across all regimes. Ultimately, our framework effectively combines biased model predictions and limited human labels into rigorous scientific measurements.

</details>


### [133] [Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17585)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,You Wu,Ling Liang,Wei Peng,Athanasios V. Vasilakos,Qingyu Zhao,Yu Zhang,Ehsan Adeli,Kilian M. Pohl,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了像素空间扩散Transformer（pDiTs），探讨其在模型架构、连续生成机制和统一多模态建模方面的进展，旨在克服潜空间扩散模型的信息损失和训练目标不匹配问题，实现高保真、端到端的视觉基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型（LDMs）在VAE压缩潜空间中存在细节信息丢失（如纹理、结构），且表示学习与扩散训练分离导致生成目标不匹配，因此需要重新探索像素空间扩散以支持端到端优化和高保真生成。

Method: 回顾像素空间扩散Transformer（pDiTs），从模型架构设计、连续生成机制（如噪声调度、损失加权）以及统一多模态建模（将像素、文本、任务条件映射到共享Token空间）三个角度进行系统综述。

Result: 总结了代表性pDiTs方法，识别了高维建模中的关键技术挑战（噪声调度、Token效率、可扩展架构），并展示了像素空间模型在统一视觉理解与生成方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 像素空间扩散为高保真、端到端的视觉基础模型提供了有前景的方向，未来需要进一步解决高维生成效率问题，并推动生成与理解深度融合的统一框架。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) enable efficient high-resolution image synthesis by denoising in a VAE-compressed latent space. However, fixed visual tokenizers can discard fine textures and structural details, while separate representation and diffusion training creates a mismatch between reconstruction and generation objectives. These limitations have renewed interest in pixel-space diffusion, which models raw pixels directly, removes the VAE bottleneck, and supports end-to-end optimization. This formulation better matches the demands of high-fidelity generation but introduces challenges in high-dimensional modeling, including noise scheduling, loss weighting, token efficiency, and scalable architecture design. Pixel-space modeling also offers a promising basis for unified multimodal systems: raw pixels, text, and task conditions can be represented in a shared token space and jointly processed by a single Transformer, narrowing the gap between visual understanding and generation. This paper reviews Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers (pDiTs) from the perspectives of model architecture, continuous generative mechanisms, and unified multimodal modeling. We summarize representative methods, identify key technical challenges, and discuss future directions toward high-fidelity, end-to-end vision foundation models that integrate generation and understanding.

</details>


### [134] [Miles: Metric Learning with Expandable Subspace for Pre-Trained Model-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17593)
*Kai Jiang,Zisong Lin,Hongyuan Zhang,Xueru Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Miles方法，通过度量学习和可扩展子空间，在预训练模型基础上实现高效的类增量学习，在六种基准数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练模型的类增量学习方法无法在性能和计算开销之间取得平衡：要么使用相同参数空间导致灾难性遗忘，要么为每个任务扩展新分支增加计算成本。

Method: 将可学习模块与预训练模型解耦，利用骨干网络中间特征的先验信息实现灵活参数扩展；采用中心损失引导新类别在新任务子空间中向对应原型聚类，并加入辅助距离正则化项以保持跨任务的度量平衡。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，Miles在各种类增量学习设置下均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Miles通过度量学习和可扩展子空间方法，有效利用预训练知识，实现了参数的高效扩展，在类增量学习中取得了性能与计算开销的良好平衡。

Abstract: Class Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to learn new concepts consistently from a data stream without forgetting. Unlike typical CIL methods which need to learn a model from scratch, pre-trained model (PTM) can easily adapt to a new task with fine-tuning. However, existing PTM-based CIL methods fail to achieve a trade-off between performance and computational expenditure, i.e., they either adopt the same parameter space so that leading catastrophic forgetting, or expand a new branch for each task but adding more computational cost. To this end, we propose MetrIc Learning with Expandable Subspace (Miles) to harness the prior information within pre-trained knowledge, thereby orchestrating an efficient expansion of the parameter space through guided optimization. Specifically, it decouples the learnable modules with the pre-trained model, exploiting prior information from intermediate features of the backbone network to enable more flexible parameter expansion. Then, a central loss is adopted to guide the new category to cluster towards the corresponding prototype in the new task subspace while incorporating an auxiliary distance regularization term to maintain metric equilibrium across tasks. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets demonstrate that Miles achieves state-of-the-art performance in various CIL settings.

</details>


### [135] [ConsiSpace: Learning Geometric Consistency Matters for Video Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17599)
*Ting Huang,Zhenyu Zhang,Wenyuan Huang,Jian Yang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConsiSpace是一个几何一致性感知框架，通过构建几何一致记忆(GCM)和统一一致性自监督强化学习(UC-SSRL)，有效提升了多模态大模型在视频空间推理任务中的表现，在三个基准上平均提升12.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs偏向语义理解，难以在长视频多视角下可靠聚合一致的空间证据，导致推理效率低且不稳定。

Method: 提出ConsiSpace框架：(1) 几何一致记忆(GCM)，包含隐式证据token和显式几何线索；(2) 统一一致性自监督强化学习(UC-SSRL)，在SFT后优化跨视角稳定性，包括答案、度量、拓扑三个一致性奖励。

Result: 在VSI-Bench、OSI-Bench、MMSI-Video-Bench三个基准上取得一致提升，平均超过最强基线12.6分。

Conclusion: 将空间一致性同时作为证据组织原则和后SFT学习信号，能有效提升视频空间推理的几何敏感性和跨视角稳定性。

Abstract: Video spatial reasoning is essential for navigation-oriented perception and long-video question answering, where models must infer spatial relations across long horizons under changing viewpoints. However, existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain largely semantic-centric, and often fail to reliably aggregate consistent spatial evidence from redundant video observations, leading to inefficient or unstable reasoning. To address these issues, we propose ConsiSpace, a geometry-consistency-aware framework for geometry-sensitive video spatial reasoning that turns spatial consistency into both an evidence organization principle and an explicit post-SFT learning signal. We build a geometry-consistent memory (GCM) including implicit evidence tokens and explicit geometric cues, and leverage efficient organization strategies to compactly preserve task-related spatial evidence.
